// === FILE_START: server/models/Fee.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export type FeePaymentMethod =
  | 'Cash'
  | 'Bank Deposit'
  | 'Bank Transfer'
  | 'Easypaisa / JazzCash'
  | 'Online Slip';

export type FeeVoucherStatus = 'Paid' | 'Unpaid' | 'Partial' | 'Overdue';

export interface IFeePaymentHistory {
  receipt_no: string;
  date: string;
  amount: number;
  method: FeePaymentMethod;
  transaction_id?: string;
  notes?: string;
}

export interface IFee extends Document {
  voucher_id?: string;
  student_id: string;
  student_name: string;
  class_name: string;
  fee_month: string;
  fee_month_key?: string;
  year: number;
  tuition_fee: number;
  admission_fee: number;
  other_charges: number;
  total_amount: number;
  paid_amount: number;
  pending_amount: number;
  due_date: string;
  status: FeeVoucherStatus;
  payment_history: IFeePaymentHistory[];
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface FeePaymentHistoryDto {
  receipt_no: string;
  date: string;
  amount: number;
  method: FeePaymentMethod;
  transaction_id?: string;
  notes?: string;
}

export interface FeeDto {
  id: string;
  student_id: string;
  student_name: string;
  class_name: string;
  fee_month: string;
  year: number;
  tuition_fee: number;
  admission_fee: number;
  other_charges: number;
  total_amount: number;
  paid_amount: number;
  pending_amount: number;
  due_date: string;
  status: FeeVoucherStatus;
  payment_history: FeePaymentHistoryDto[];
}

const FeeSchema = new Schema<IFee>(
  {
    voucher_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    student_id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
    },
    student_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    class_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    fee_month: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    fee_month_key: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      lowercase: true,
    },
    year: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
    },
    tuition_fee: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
      min: [0, 'Tuition fee cannot be negative'],
    },
    admission_fee: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
      min: [0, 'Admission fee cannot be negative'],
    },
    other_charges: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
      min: [0, 'Other charges cannot be negative'],
    },
    total_amount: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: [0, 'Total fee amount cannot be negative'],
    },
    paid_amount: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
      min: [0, 'Paid amount cannot be negative'],
    },
    pending_amount: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
      min: [0, 'Pending amount cannot be negative'],
    },
    due_date: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
    },
    status: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['Paid', 'Unpaid', 'Partial', 'Overdue'],
      default: 'Unpaid',
    },
    payment_history: [
      {
        receipt_no: { type: String, required: true },
        date: { type: String, required: true },
        amount: { type: Number, required: true, min: [1, 'Payment amount must be greater than zero'] },
        method: { type: String, default: 'Cash' },
        transaction_id: { type: String, default: '' },
        notes: { type: String, default: '' },
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

FeeSchema.pre('validate', function () {
  if (this.fee_month && !this.fee_month_key) {
    this.fee_month_key = String(this.fee_month).trim().toLowerCase();
  }
});

FeeSchema.index({ voucher_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
FeeSchema.index({ student_id: 1, fee_month: 1, year: 1 }, { unique: true });
FeeSchema.index({ student_id: 1, fee_month_key: 1, year: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
FeeSchema.index({ student_id: 1, status: 1 });
FeeSchema.index({ status: 1, fee_month: 1, year: 1 });
FeeSchema.index({ class_name: 1, status: 1 });

export function toFeeDto(doc: any): FeeDto {
  return {
    id: String(doc.voucher_id || doc.record_id || doc.id || (doc._id ? doc._id.toString() : '')),
    student_id: String(doc.student_id || ''),
    student_name: String(doc.student_name || ''),
    class_name: String(doc.class_name || ''),
    fee_month: String(doc.fee_month || ''),
    year: Number(doc.year || 2026),
    tuition_fee: Number(doc.tuition_fee ?? 0),
    admission_fee: Number(doc.admission_fee ?? 0),
    other_charges: Number(doc.other_charges ?? 0),
    total_amount: Number(doc.total_amount ?? 0),
    paid_amount: Number(doc.paid_amount ?? 0),
    pending_amount: Number(doc.pending_amount ?? 0),
    due_date: String(doc.due_date || ''),
    status: (doc.status || 'Unpaid') as FeeVoucherStatus,
    payment_history: Array.isArray(doc.payment_history)
      ? doc.payment_history.map((p: any) => ({
          receipt_no: String(p.receipt_no || ''),
          date: String(p.date || ''),
          amount: Number(p.amount || 0),
          method: (p.method || 'Cash') as FeePaymentMethod,
          ...(p.transaction_id ? { transaction_id: String(p.transaction_id) } : {}),
          ...(p.notes !== undefined ? { notes: String(p.notes ?? '') } : {}),
        }))
      : [],
  };
}

export const FeeModel = mongoose.models.Fee || mongoose.model<IFee>('Fee', FeeSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/Fee.ts ===

// === FILE_START: server/models/FeePayment.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';
import type { FeePaymentMethod } from './Fee.ts';

export interface IFeePayment extends Document {
  fee_id: string;
  student_id: string;
  receipt_no: string;
  transaction_id?: string;
  date: string;
  amount: number;
  method: FeePaymentMethod;
  notes?: string;
  received_by?: string;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface FeePaymentDto {
  id: string;
  fee_id: string;
  student_id: string;
  receipt_no: string;
  transaction_id?: string;
  date: string;
  amount: number;
  method: FeePaymentMethod;
  notes: string;
  received_by: string;
}

const FeePaymentSchema = new Schema<IFeePayment>(
  {
    fee_id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
    },
    student_id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
    },
    receipt_no: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
    },
    transaction_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      set: (val: unknown) => {
        if (typeof val !== 'string') return undefined;
        const clean = val.trim();
        return clean ? clean : undefined;
      },
    },
    date: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
    },
    amount: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: [1, 'Payment amount must be greater than zero'],
    },
    method: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['Cash', 'Bank Deposit', 'Bank Transfer', 'Easypaisa / JazzCash', 'Online Slip'],
      default: 'Cash',
    },
    notes: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
    received_by: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

FeePaymentSchema.index({ student_id: 1, date: -1 });
FeePaymentSchema.index({ fee_id: 1 });
FeePaymentSchema.index({ transaction_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });

export function toFeePaymentDto(doc: any): FeePaymentDto {
  return {
    id: String(doc._id ? doc._id.toString() : ''),
    fee_id: String(doc.fee_id || ''),
    student_id: String(doc.student_id || ''),
    receipt_no: String(doc.receipt_no || ''),
    ...(doc.transaction_id ? { transaction_id: String(doc.transaction_id) } : {}),
    date: String(doc.date || ''),
    amount: Number(doc.amount || 0),
    method: (doc.method || 'Cash') as FeePaymentMethod,
    notes: String(doc.notes || ''),
    received_by: String(doc.received_by || ''),
  };
}

export const FeePaymentModel =
  mongoose.models.FeePayment || mongoose.model<IFeePayment>('FeePayment', FeePaymentSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/FeePayment.ts ===

// === FILE_START: server/models/Parent.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface IParent extends Document {
  record_id?: string;
  user_id: string;
  name: string;
  father_name?: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  address?: string;
  occupation?: string;
  linked_students: string[];
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface ParentDto {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  name: string;
  father_name: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  address: string;
  occupation: string;
  linked_students: string[];
  created_at: string;
}

const ParentSchema = new Schema<IParent>(
  {
    record_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    user_id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    father_name: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    phone: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    email: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    address: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    occupation: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    linked_students: {
      type: [String],
      default: [],
      index: true,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

ParentSchema.index({ record_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });

export function toParentDto(doc: any): ParentDto {
  const id = String(doc?.record_id || doc?.id || doc?._id || '');
  const createdRaw = doc?.created_at;
  const createdIso =
    createdRaw instanceof Date
      ? createdRaw.toISOString()
      : typeof createdRaw === 'string' && createdRaw
      ? createdRaw
      : new Date().toISOString();

  return {
    id,
    user_id: String(doc?.user_id || ''),
    name: String(doc?.name || ''),
    father_name: String(doc?.father_name || ''),
    phone: String(doc?.phone || ''),
    email: String(doc?.email || '').toLowerCase(),
    address: String(doc?.address || ''),
    occupation: String(doc?.occupation || ''),
    linked_students: Array.isArray(doc?.linked_students)
      ? doc.linked_students.map((s: unknown) => String(s)).filter(Boolean)
      : [],
    created_at: createdIso,
  };
}

export const ParentModel = mongoose.models.Parent || mongoose.model<IParent>('Parent', ParentSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/Parent.ts ===

// === FILE_START: server/models/PasswordResetToken.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, type Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface IPasswordResetToken extends Document {
  token_hash: string;
  user_id: string;
  email: string;
  expires_at: Date;
  used_at: Date | null;
  request_ip?: string;
  created_at: Date;
}

const PasswordResetTokenSchema = new Schema<IPasswordResetToken>(
  {
    token_hash: { type: String, required: true, unique: true, index: true },
    user_id: { type: String, required: true, index: true },
    email: { type: String, required: true, lowercase: true, trim: true },
    // Enforce 15-minute validity in queries; TTL index purges expired documents automatically after 1 hour
    expires_at: { type: Date, required: true, index: { expireAfterSeconds: 3600 } },
    used_at: { type: Date, default: null },
    request_ip: { type: String, default: '' },
    created_at: { type: Date, default: Date.now },
  },
  {
    collection: 'password_reset_tokens',
    versionKey: false,
  }
);

export const PasswordResetTokenModel =
  mongoose.models.PasswordResetToken ||
  mongoose.model<IPasswordResetToken>('PasswordResetToken', PasswordResetTokenSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/PasswordResetToken.ts ===

// === FILE_START: server/models/RateLimitBucket.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, type Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface IRateLimitBucket extends Document {
  bucket_key: string;
  count: number;
  reset_at: Date;
}

const RateLimitBucketSchema = new Schema<IRateLimitBucket>(
  {
    bucket_key: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
    count: { type: Number, required: true, default: 1 },
    reset_at: { type: Date, required: true, index: { expireAfterSeconds: 0 } },
  },
  {
    collection: 'rate_limit_buckets',
    versionKey: false,
  }
);

export const RateLimitBucketModel =
  mongoose.models.RateLimitBucket ||
  mongoose.model<IRateLimitBucket>('RateLimitBucket', RateLimitBucketSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/RateLimitBucket.ts ===

// === FILE_START: server/models/Result.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface IResultSubject {
  subject_name: string;
  total_marks: number;
  obtained_marks: number;
  grade: string;
}

export interface IResult extends Document {
  record_id?: string;
  exam_id: string;
  exam_title: string;
  student_id: string;
  student_name: string;
  class_name: string;
  section: string;
  subjects: IResultSubject[];
  total_marks: number;
  obtained_marks: number;
  percentage: number;
  overall_grade: string;
  remarks?: string;
  published: boolean;
  academic_session?: string;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface ResultSubjectDto {
  subject_name: string;
  total_marks: number;
  obtained_marks: number;
  grade: string;
}

export interface ResultDto {
  id: string;
  exam_id: string;
  exam_title: string;
  student_id: string;
  student_name: string;
  class_name: string;
  section: string;
  subjects: ResultSubjectDto[];
  total_marks: number;
  obtained_marks: number;
  percentage: number;
  overall_grade: string;
  remarks: string;
  published: boolean;
  academic_session?: string;
}

const ResultSchema = new Schema<IResult>(
  {
    record_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    exam_id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      index: true,
    },
    exam_title: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    student_id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      index: true,
    },
    student_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    class_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    section: {
      type: String,
      default: 'A',
    },
    subjects: [
      {
        subject_name: { type: String, required: true },
        total_marks: { type: Number, required: true, min: [1, 'Total marks must be positive'] },
        obtained_marks: { type: Number, required: true, min: [0, 'Obtained marks cannot be negative'] },
        grade: { type: String, default: 'A' },
      },
    ],
    total_marks: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: [1, 'Total marks must be positive'],
    },
    obtained_marks: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: [0, 'Obtained marks cannot be negative'],
    },
    percentage: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: [0, 'Percentage cannot be negative'],
      max: [100, 'Percentage cannot exceed 100'],
    },
    overall_grade: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
    },
    remarks: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
    published: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: true,
      index: true,
    },
    academic_session: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

ResultSchema.index({ record_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
ResultSchema.index({ exam_id: 1, student_id: 1 }, { unique: true });
ResultSchema.index({ class_name: 1, exam_id: 1 });
ResultSchema.index({ student_id: 1, published: 1 });

export function toResultDto(doc: any): ResultDto {
  return {
    id: String(doc.record_id || doc.id || (doc._id ? doc._id.toString() : '')),
    exam_id: String(doc.exam_id || ''),
    exam_title: String(doc.exam_title || ''),
    student_id: String(doc.student_id || ''),
    student_name: String(doc.student_name || ''),
    class_name: String(doc.class_name || ''),
    section: String(doc.section || 'A'),
    subjects: Array.isArray(doc.subjects)
      ? doc.subjects.map((s: any) => ({
          subject_name: String(s.subject_name || ''),
          total_marks: Number(s.total_marks || 0),
          obtained_marks: Number(s.obtained_marks ?? 0),
          grade: String(s.grade || 'F'),
        }))
      : [],
    total_marks: Number(doc.total_marks || 0),
    obtained_marks: Number(doc.obtained_marks ?? 0),
    percentage: Number(doc.percentage ?? 0),
    overall_grade: String(doc.overall_grade || 'F'),
    remarks: String(doc.remarks ?? ''),
    published: doc.published !== false,
    ...(doc.academic_session ? { academic_session: String(doc.academic_session) } : {}),
  };
}

export const ResultModel = mongoose.models.Result || mongoose.model<IResult>('Result', ResultSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/Result.ts ===

// === FILE_START: server/models/RevokedToken.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, type Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface IRevokedToken extends Document {
  token_hash: string;
  expires_at: Date;
  revoked_at: Date;
  reason?: string;
}

const RevokedTokenSchema = new Schema<IRevokedToken>(
  {
    token_hash: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
    expires_at: { type: Date, required: true, index: { expireAfterSeconds: 0 } },
    revoked_at: { type: Date, default: Date.now },
    reason: { type: String, default: 'logout' },
  },
  {
    collection: 'revoked_tokens',
    versionKey: false,
  }
);

export const RevokedTokenModel =
  mongoose.models.RevokedToken || mongoose.model<IRevokedToken>('RevokedToken', RevokedTokenSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/RevokedToken.ts ===

const INTERNAL_SETTINGS_KEYS = new Set([
  '_id',
  '__v',
  'singleton_key',
  'created_at',
  'updated_at',
  '__proto__',
  'constructor',
  'prototype',
]);

export function toSchoolSettingsDto(doc: any): Record<string, any> {
  const raw =
    doc && typeof doc.toObject === 'function'
      ? doc.toObject({ flattenMaps: true })
      : doc && typeof doc === 'object'
      ? { ...doc }
      : {};

  const result: Record<string, any> = {
    ...DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS,
  };

  for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(raw)) {
    if (INTERNAL_SETTINGS_KEYS.has(key) || key.startsWith('$')) {
      continue;
    }
    if (val !== undefined) {
      result[key] = val;
    }
  }

  result.core_values = Array.isArray(result.core_values)
    ? result.core_values.map((v: any) => String(v ?? ''))
    : [...DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.core_values];

  result.features = Array.isArray(result.features)
    ? result.features.map((f: any) => ({
        title: String(f?.title ?? ''),
        description: String(f?.description ?? ''),
        icon: String(f?.icon ?? 'Award'),
      }))
    : DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.features.map(f => ({ ...f }));

  result.academic_levels = Array.isArray(result.academic_levels)
    ? result.academic_levels.map((l: any) => ({
        level: String(l?.level ?? ''),
        grades: String(l?.grades ?? ''),
        description: String(l?.description ?? ''),
        stream: String(l?.stream ?? ''),
      }))
    : DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.academic_levels.map(l => ({ ...l }));

  result.facilities = Array.isArray(result.facilities)
    ? result.facilities.map((fac: any) => ({
        name: String(fac?.name ?? ''),
        description: String(fac?.description ?? ''),
        image: String(fac?.image ?? ''),
      }))
    : DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.facilities.map(f => ({ ...f }));

  result.testimonials = Array.isArray(result.testimonials)
    ? result.testimonials.map((t: any) => ({
        name: String(t?.name ?? ''),
        role: String(t?.role ?? ''),
        quote: String(t?.quote ?? ''),
        year: String(t?.year ?? ''),
      }))
    : DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.testimonials.map(t => ({ ...t }));

  return result;
}

const FeatureItemSchema = new Schema<ISchoolFeatureItem>(
  {
    title: { type: String, default: '' },
    description: { type: String, default: '' },
    icon: { type: String, default: 'Award' },
  },
  { _id: false }
);

const AcademicLevelItemSchema = new Schema<ISchoolAcademicLevelItem>(
  {
    level: { type: String, default: '' },
    grades: { type: String, default: '' },
    description: { type: String, default: '' },
    stream: { type: String, default: '' },
  },
  { _id: false }
);

const FacilityItemSchema = new Schema<ISchoolFacilityItem>(
  {
    name: { type: String, default: '' },
    description: { type: String, default: '' },
    image: { type: String, default: '' },
  },
  { _id: false }
);

const TestimonialItemSchema = new Schema<ISchoolTestimonialItem>(
  {
    name: { type: String, default: '' },
    role: { type: String, default: '' },
    quote: { type: String, default: '' },
    year: { type: String, default: '' },
  },
  { _id: false }
);

const SchoolSettingsSchema = new Schema<ISchoolSettings>(
  {
    singleton_key: {
      type: String,
      default: 'default',
      unique: true,
      index: true,
    },
    school_name: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.school_name },
    short_name: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.short_name },
    slogan: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.slogan },
    location: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.location },
    google_maps_code: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.google_maps_code },
    google_maps_embed_query: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.google_maps_embed_query },
    phone: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.phone },
    alt_phone: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.alt_phone },
    email: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.email },
    admission_email: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.admission_email },
    affiliation: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.affiliation },
    registration_info: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.registration_info },
    school_timings_summer: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.school_timings_summer },
    school_timings_winter: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.school_timings_winter },
    principal_name: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.principal_name },
    principal_message: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.principal_message },
    about_text: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.about_text },
    mission: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.mission },
    vision: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.vision },
    core_values: { type: [String], default: () => [...DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.core_values] },
    social_facebook: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.social_facebook },
    social_whatsapp: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.social_whatsapp },
    hero_badge: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.hero_badge },
    hero_title: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.hero_title },
    hero_subtitle: { type: String, default: DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.hero_subtitle },
    features: { type: [FeatureItemSchema], default: () => DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.features.map(f => ({ ...f })) },
    academic_levels: {
      type: [AcademicLevelItemSchema],
      default: () => DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.academic_levels.map(l => ({ ...l })),
    },
    facilities: { type: [FacilityItemSchema], default: () => DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.facilities.map(f => ({ ...f })) },
    testimonials: {
      type: [TestimonialItemSchema],
      default: () => DEFAULT_SCHOOL_SETTINGS.testimonials.map(t => ({ ...t })),
    },
  },
  {
    strict: false,
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

export const SchoolSettingsModel =
  mongoose.models.SchoolSettings || mongoose.model<ISchoolSettings>('SchoolSettings', SchoolSettingsSchema);
// === FILE_END: server/models/SchoolSettings.ts ===

// === FILE_START: server/models/Student.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface IStudent extends Document {
  record_id?: string;
  admission_no: string;
  roll_no: string;
  full_name: string;
  father_name: string;
  dob: string;
  gender: 'Male' | 'Female' | 'Other';
  class_name: string;
  section: string;
  contact_no: string;
  address: string;
  emergency_contact: string;
  profile_image?: string;
  admission_date: string;
  academic_status: 'Active' | 'Inactive' | 'Graduated' | 'Suspended';
  parent_user_id?: string;
  user_id?: string;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface StudentDto {
  id: string;
  admission_no: string;
  roll_no: string;
  full_name: string;
  father_name: string;
  dob: string;
  gender: 'Male' | 'Female' | 'Other';
  class_name: string;
  section: string;
  contact_no: string;
  address: string;
  emergency_contact: string;
  profile_image?: string;
  admission_date: string;
  academic_status: 'Active' | 'Inactive' | 'Graduated' | 'Suspended';
  parent_user_id?: string;
  user_id?: string;
}

const StudentSchema = new Schema<IStudent>(
  {
    record_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    admission_no: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    roll_no: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    full_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    father_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    dob: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
    gender: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['Male', 'Female', 'Other'],
      required: true,
    },
    class_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    section: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      default: 'A',
    },
    contact_no: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    address: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    emergency_contact: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    profile_image: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
    admission_date: {
      type: String,
      default: () => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    },
    academic_status: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['Active', 'Inactive', 'Graduated', 'Suspended'],
      default: 'Active',
      index: true,
    },
    parent_user_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    user_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

StudentSchema.index({ record_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
StudentSchema.index({ user_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
StudentSchema.index({ class_name: 1, section: 1, roll_no: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
StudentSchema.index({ class_name: 1, academic_status: 1 });
StudentSchema.index({ parent_user_id: 1 });

export function toStudentDto(doc: any): StudentDto {
  const id = String(doc?.record_id || doc?.id || doc?._id || '');
  return {
    id,
    admission_no: String(doc?.admission_no || ''),
    roll_no: String(doc?.roll_no || ''),
    full_name: String(doc?.full_name || ''),
    father_name: String(doc?.father_name || ''),
    dob: doc?.dob !== undefined && doc?.dob !== null ? String(doc.dob) : '',
    gender: (doc?.gender === 'Female' || doc?.gender === 'Other' ? doc.gender : 'Male') as 'Male' | 'Female' | 'Other',
    class_name: String(doc?.class_name || ''),
    section: String(doc?.section || 'A'),
    contact_no: String(doc?.contact_no || ''),
    address: String(doc?.address || ''),
    emergency_contact: String(doc?.emergency_contact || ''),
    ...(doc?.profile_image ? { profile_image: String(doc.profile_image) } : {}),
    admission_date: String(doc?.admission_date || ''),
    academic_status: (doc?.academic_status || 'Active') as 'Active' | 'Inactive' | 'Graduated' | 'Suspended',
    ...(doc?.parent_user_id ? { parent_user_id: String(doc.parent_user_id) } : {}),
    ...(doc?.user_id ? { user_id: String(doc.user_id) } : {}),
  };
}

export const StudentModel = mongoose.models.Student || mongoose.model<IStudent>('Student', StudentSchema);

import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface ISubject extends Document {
  record_id?: string;
  class_name: string;
  name: string;
  code: string;
  teacher_name: string;
  weekly_periods: number;
  status?: 'Active' | 'Inactive';
  max_marks?: number;
  passing_marks?: number;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

const SubjectSchema = new Schema<ISubject>(
  {
    record_id: {
      type: String,
      unique: true,
      sparse: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    class_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    code: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    teacher_name: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    weekly_periods: {
      type: Number,
      default: 5,
      min: 1,
    },
    status: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['Active', 'Inactive'],
      default: 'Active',
    },
    max_marks: {
      type: Number,
      min: 1,
    },
    passing_marks: {
      type: Number,
      min: 0,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

SubjectSchema.index({ class_name: 1, name: 1 }, { unique: true });

export function toSubjectDto(doc: any) {
  return {
    id: doc.record_id || String(doc._id),
    class_name: doc.class_name,
    name: doc.name,
    code: doc.code ?? '',
    teacher_name: doc.teacher_name ?? '',
    weekly_periods:
      typeof doc.weekly_periods === 'number'
        ? doc.weekly_periods
        : Number(doc.weekly_periods) || 5,
    status: doc.status || 'Active',
    ...(typeof doc.max_marks === 'number' ? { max_marks: doc.max_marks } : {}),
    ...(typeof doc.passing_marks === 'number' ? { passing_marks: doc.passing_marks } : {}),
  };
}

import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface ITeacher extends Document {
  record_id?: string;
  user_id?: string;
  name: string;
  profile_photo?: string;
  qualification: string;
  subject: string;
  classes: string[];
  contact_no: string;
  email?: string;
  joining_date: string;
  experience: string;
  status: 'Active' | 'On Leave' | 'Resigned';
  bio: string;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface TeacherDto {
  id: string;
  user_id?: string;
  name: string;
  profile_photo: string;
  qualification: string;
  subject: string;
  classes: string[];
  contact_no: string;
  email: string;
  joining_date: string;
  experience: string;
  status: 'Active' | 'On Leave' | 'Resigned';
  bio: string;
}

const TeacherSchema = new Schema<ITeacher>(
  {
    record_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    user_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    profile_photo: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
    qualification: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    subject: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    classes: {
      type: [String],
      default: [],
    },
    contact_no: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    email: {
      type: String,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    joining_date: {
      type: String,
      default: () => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    },
    experience: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
    status: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['Active', 'On Leave', 'Resigned'],
      default: 'Active',
      index: true,
    },
    bio: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

TeacherSchema.index({ record_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
TeacherSchema.index({ email: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });

export function toTeacherDto(doc: any): TeacherDto {
  const id = String(doc?.record_id || doc?.id || doc?._id || '');
  return {
    id,
    ...(doc?.user_id ? { user_id: String(doc.user_id) } : {}),
    name: String(doc?.name || ''),
    profile_photo: String(doc?.profile_photo || ''),
    qualification: String(doc?.qualification || ''),
    subject: String(doc?.subject || ''),
    classes: Array.isArray(doc?.classes) ? doc.classes.map((c: unknown) => String(c)).filter(Boolean) : [],
    contact_no: String(doc?.contact_no || ''),
    email: doc?.email ? String(doc.email).toLowerCase() : '',
    joining_date: String(doc?.joining_date || ''),
    experience: String(doc?.experience || ''),
    status: (doc?.status === 'On Leave' || doc?.status === 'Resigned' ? doc.status : 'Active') as
      | 'Active'
      | 'On Leave'
      | 'Resigned',
    bio: String(doc?.bio || ''),
  };
}

export const TeacherModel = mongoose.models.Teacher || mongoose.model<ITeacher>('Teacher', TeacherSchema);

import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export interface ITeacherAssignment extends Document {
  record_id?: string;
  teacher_id: string;
  teacher_name: string;
  class_name: string;
  section: string;
  subject: string;
  academic_session: string;
  assigned_date: string;
  is_class_teacher?: boolean;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface TeacherAssignmentDto {
  id: string;
  teacher_id: string;
  teacher_name: string;
  class_name: string;
  section: string;
  subject: string;
  academic_session: string;
  assigned_date: string;
  is_class_teacher?: boolean;
}

const TeacherAssignmentSchema = new Schema<ITeacherAssignment>(
  {
    record_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    teacher_id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    teacher_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    class_name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    section: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    subject: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
    },
    academic_session: {
      type: String,
      default: '2026-2027',
      trim: true,
    },
    assigned_date: {
      type: String,
      default: () => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    },
    is_class_teacher: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
  }
);

TeacherAssignmentSchema.index({ record_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });
TeacherAssignmentSchema.index(
  { teacher_id: 1, class_name: 1, section: 1, subject: 1, academic_session: 1 },
  { unique: true }
);

export function toTeacherAssignmentDto(doc: any): TeacherAssignmentDto {
  const id = String(doc?.record_id || doc?.id || doc?._id || '');
  const fallbackDate =
    doc?.created_at instanceof Date ? doc.created_at.toISOString().split('T')[0] : '';
  return {
    id,
    teacher_id: String(doc?.teacher_id || ''),
    teacher_name: String(doc?.teacher_name || ''),
    class_name: String(doc?.class_name || ''),
    section: String(doc?.section || 'A'),
    subject: String(doc?.subject || ''),
    academic_session: String(doc?.academic_session || '2026-2027'),
    assigned_date: String(doc?.assigned_date || fallbackDate),
    ...(doc?.is_class_teacher !== undefined ? { is_class_teacher: Boolean(doc.is_class_teacher) } : {}),
  };
}

// === FILE_START: server/models/User.ts ===
import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';

export type UserRole = 'super_admin' | 'admin' | 'teacher' | 'parent' | 'student';
export type UserStatus = 'active' | 'inactive' | 'pending' | 'suspended';

export interface IUser extends Document {
  record_id?: string;
  email: string;
  password_hash: string;
  name: string;
  role: UserRole;
  phone?: string;
  avatar?: string;
  linked_id?: string;
  linked_student_ids?: string[];
  status: UserStatus;
  password_changed_at?: Date;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface UserDto {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  role: UserRole;
  phone?: string;
  avatar?: string;
  linked_id?: string;
  linked_student_ids?: string[];
  status: UserStatus;
  created_at: string;
}

const stripSensitiveUserFields = (_doc: unknown, ret: Record<string, any>) => {
  delete ret.password_hash;
  delete ret.password;
  delete ret.password_changed_at;
  delete ret.__v;
  return ret;
};

const UserSchema = new Schema<IUser>(
  {
    record_id: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
    email: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, 'Email is required'],
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    password_hash: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, 'Password hash is required'],
    },
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: [true, 'Name is required'],
      trim: true,
    },
    role: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['super_admin', 'admin', 'teacher', 'parent', 'student'],
      default: 'student',
      index: true,
    },
    phone: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      default: '',
    },
    avatar: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
    },
    linked_id: {
      type: String,
      default: '',
      index: true,
    },
    linked_student_ids: {
      type: [String],
      default: [],
    },
    status: {
      type: String,
      enum: ['active', 'inactive', 'pending', 'suspended'],
      default: 'active',
      index: true,
    },
    password_changed_at: {
      type: Date,
      default: null,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: { createdAt: 'created_at', updatedAt: 'updated_at' },
    toJSON: {
      transform: stripSensitiveUserFields,
    },
    toObject: {
      transform: stripSensitiveUserFields,
    },
  }
);

UserSchema.index({ record_id: 1 }, { unique: true, sparse: true });

export function toUserDto(doc: any): UserDto {
  const id = String(doc?.record_id || doc?.id || doc?._id || '');
  const createdRaw = doc?.created_at;
  const createdIso =
    createdRaw instanceof Date
      ? createdRaw.toISOString()
      : typeof createdRaw === 'string' && createdRaw
      ? createdRaw
      : new Date().toISOString();

  return {
    id,
    email: String(doc?.email || '').toLowerCase(),
    name: String(doc?.name || ''),
    role: (doc?.role || 'student') as UserRole,
    phone: doc?.phone !== undefined ? String(doc.phone) : '',
    ...(doc?.avatar ? { avatar: String(doc.avatar) } : {}),
    ...(doc?.linked_id ? { linked_id: String(doc.linked_id) } : {}),
    linked_student_ids: Array.isArray(doc?.linked_student_ids)
      ? doc.linked_student_ids.map((s: unknown) => String(s)).filter(Boolean)
      : [],
    status: (doc?.status || 'active') as UserStatus,
    created_at: createdIso,
  };
}

export const UserModel = mongoose.models.User || mongoose.model<IUser>('User', UserSchema);

    ? path.resolve(process.env.UPLOAD_STORAGE_DIR)

function ensureMongoReady(res: Response): boolean {
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState !== 1) {
    res.status(503).json({
      error: 'Database service temporarily unavailable. Write/read aborted safely without modifying local fallback state.',
    });
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function isMongoUnavailableError(err: any): boolean {
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState !== 1) return true;
  const name = String(err?.name || '');
  if (
    name === 'MongoNetworkError' ||
    name === 'MongoServerSelectionError' ||
    name === 'MongooseServerSelectionError' ||
    name === 'MongoNotConnectedError' ||
    name === 'MongoTopologyClosedError' ||
    name === 'MongoPoolClearedError'
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  const msg = String(err?.message || '');
  return /topology.*closed|client must be connected|not connected|ECONNREFUSED|server selection timed out|buffering timed out/i.test(
    msg
  );
}

function sendRouteError(res: Response, err: any, fallbackMessage: string) {
  if (isMongoUnavailableError(err)) {
    return res.status(503).json({
      error: 'Database service temporarily unavailable. Write/read aborted safely without modifying local fallback state.',
    });
  }
  return res.status(500).json({
    error: sanitizeErrorMessage(err?.message || fallbackMessage),
  });
}

function escapeRegexLiteral(input: string): string {
  return input.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
}

function buildAdmissionLookupFilter(rawId: string): Record<string, any> {
  const trimmed = String(rawId || '').trim();
  const clauses: Record<string, any>[] = [
    { record_id: trimmed },
    { application_no: trimmed.toUpperCase() },
  ];
  if (mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(trimmed) && /^[a-fA-F0-9]{24}$/.test(trimmed)) {
    clauses.push({ _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(trimmed) });
  }
  return { $or: clauses };
}

function isValidCmsIdParam(rawId: unknown): boolean {
  if (typeof rawId !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = rawId.trim();
  return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{2,80}$/.test(trimmed);
}

function buildCmsRecordLookupFilter(rawId: string): Record<string, any> {
  const trimmed = String(rawId || '').trim();
  const clauses: Record<string, any>[] = [{ record_id: trimmed }];
  if (mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(trimmed) && /^[a-fA-F0-9]{24}$/.test(trimmed)) {
    clauses.push({ _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(trimmed) });
  }
  return clauses.length === 1 ? clauses[0] : { $or: clauses };
}

function buildFeeLookupFilter(rawId: string): Record<string, any> {
  const trimmed = String(rawId || '').trim();
  const clauses: Record<string, any>[] = [{ voucher_id: trimmed }];
  if (mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(trimmed) && /^[a-fA-F0-9]{24}$/.test(trimmed)) {
    clauses.push({ _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(trimmed) });
  }
  return clauses.length === 1 ? clauses[0] : { $or: clauses };
}

const VALID_TIMETABLE_DAYS = ['Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday'] as const;
const VALID_EXAM_STATUSES = ['Scheduled', 'Ongoing', 'Completed'] as const;

function isValidIsoCalendarDate(raw: unknown): boolean {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = raw.trim();
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(trimmed);
  if (!m) return false;
  const year = Number(m[1]);
  const month = Number(m[2]);
  const day = Number(m[3]);
  if (year < 1990 || year > 2100 || month < 1 || month > 12 || day < 1 || day > 31) return false;
  const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  return (
    dt.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
    dt.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
    dt.getUTCDate() === day
  );
}

function parseSchoolTimeMinutes(raw: unknown): { minutes: number; hasMeridiem: boolean } | null {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
  const trimmed = raw.trim();
  const m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?:\s*(AM|PM))?$/i.exec(trimmed);
  if (!m) return null;
  const hh = Number(m[1]);
  const mm = Number(m[2]);
  const meridiem = m[3] ? m[3].toUpperCase() : '';
  if (!Number.isInteger(hh) || !Number.isInteger(mm) || mm < 0 || mm > 59) return null;

  if (meridiem) {
    if (hh < 1 || hh > 12) return null;
    const hours24 = meridiem === 'AM' ? (hh === 12 ? 0 : hh) : (hh === 12 ? 12 : hh + 12);
    return { minutes: hours24 * 60 + mm, hasMeridiem: true };
  }

  if (hh < 0 || hh > 23) return null;
  return { minutes: hh * 60 + mm, hasMeridiem: false };
}

function parseTimetableInterval(startRaw: unknown, endRaw: unknown): { startMin: number; endMin: number } | null {
  const parsedStart = parseSchoolTimeMinutes(startRaw);
  const parsedEnd = parseSchoolTimeMinutes(endRaw);
  if (!parsedStart || !parsedEnd) return null;

  let startMin = parsedStart.minutes;
  let endMin = parsedEnd.minutes;

  // Support school-day 12-hour afternoon shorthand without AM/PM (e.g., "12:15" -> "01:00" or "01:00" -> "01:45")
  if (!parsedStart.hasMeridiem && startMin >= 60 && startMin < 420) {
    startMin += 12 * 60;
  }
  if (!parsedEnd.hasMeridiem && endMin >= 60 && endMin < 420 && startMin >= 420 && startMin < 1080) {
    endMin += 12 * 60;
  }

  if (endMin <= startMin) return null;
  return { startMin, endMin };
}

function isGenericPlaceholderTeacher(name: unknown): boolean {
  if (typeof name !== 'string') return true;
  const trimmed = name.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return true;
  return /^(?:assigned faculty|staff teacher|to be assigned|tba|n\/a|unassigned|-)$/i.test(trimmed);
}

async function resolveExistingClassDoc(rawClassName: unknown): Promise<any | null> {
  if (typeof rawClassName !== 'string') return null;
  const clean = rawClassName.trim();
  if (!clean) return null;
  return ClassModel.findOne({
    name: { $regex: new RegExp(`^${escapeRegexLiteral(clean)}$`, 'i') },
  }).lean();
}

function sendAcademicPaginatedOrArray(req: Request, res: Response, items: any[], defaultLimit = 200) {
  const total = items.length;
  const page = Math.max(1, parseInt(req.query.page as string, 10) || 1);
  const hasLimit = req.query.limit !== undefined;
  const limit = hasLimit
    ? Math.min(500, Math.max(1, parseInt(req.query.limit as string, 10) || 50))
    : defaultLimit;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / limit) || 1;

  res.setHeader('X-Total-Count', total.toString());
  res.setHeader('X-Page', page.toString());
  res.setHeader('X-Limit', limit.toString());
  res.setHeader('X-Total-Pages', totalPages.toString());

  if (req.query.paginate === 'true' || req.query.envelope === 'true') {
    const start = (page - 1) * limit;
    const paged = items.slice(start, start + limit);
    return res.json({
      data: paged,
      pagination: { total, page, limit, totalPages, hasNext: page < totalPages, hasPrev: page > 1 },
    });
  }

  if (hasLimit || req.query.page !== undefined) {
    const start = (page - 1) * limit;
    return res.json(items.slice(start, start + limit));
  }

  return res.json(items);
}

function sanitizePlainText(input: unknown, maxLen = 5000): string {
  if (input === null || input === undefined) return '';
  let str = String(input).replace(/\0/g, '');
  str = str
    .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, '')
    .replace(/<iframe\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/iframe>/gi, '')
    .replace(/<object\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/object>/gi, '')
    .replace(/<\/?(?:script|iframe|object|embed|style|link|meta|svg|math|base|form)\b[^>]*>/gi, '')
    .replace(/\bon[a-z]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]*"|'[^']*'|[^\s>]+)/gi, '')
    .replace(/javascript\s*:/gi, '');
  return str.trim().slice(0, maxLen);
}

function isSafeMediaOrWebUrl(rawUrl: unknown, allowEmpty = true): boolean {
  if (rawUrl === null || rawUrl === undefined) return allowEmpty;
  if (typeof rawUrl !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = rawUrl.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return allowEmpty;
  if (/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/.test(trimmed)) return false;

  const lower = trimmed.toLowerCase();
  if (
    lower.startsWith('javascript:') ||
    lower.startsWith('vbscript:') ||
    lower.startsWith('data:text/') ||
    lower.startsWith('data:image/svg')
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (trimmed.startsWith('/')) {
    return !trimmed.startsWith('//') && !trimmed.includes('..') && !trimmed.includes('\\');
  }

  if (/^https?:\/\/[^\s<>"]+$/i.test(trimmed)) {
    return true;
  }

  if (/^data:image\/(?:png|jpeg|jpg|webp|gif);base64,[a-zA-Z0-9+/=]+$/i.test(trimmed)) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

async function tryResolveOptionalUser(req: Request): Promise<{ id: string; role: string; name?: string } | null> {
  const token = extractTokenFromRequest(req);
  if (!token) return null;
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState !== 1) return null;
  if (await isTokenRevokedAsync(token)) return null;
  try {
    const decoded = jsonwebtoken.verify(token, getJwtSecret()) as any;
    const liveUser = await resolveLiveUserFromDecoded(decoded);
    if (!liveUser || liveUser.status !== 'active') {
      return null;
    }
    if (liveUser.password_changed_at) {
      const changedAtMs = new Date(liveUser.password_changed_at).getTime();
      const tokenIssuedMs =
        typeof decoded.iat_ms === 'number'
          ? decoded.iat_ms
          : typeof decoded.iat === 'number'
          ? decoded.iat * 1000
          : 0;
      if (!isNaN(changedAtMs) && tokenIssuedMs < changedAtMs) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    return {
      id: liveUser.record_id || (liveUser._id ? liveUser._id.toString() : ''),
      role: String(liveUser.role),
      name: liveUser.name,
    };
  } catch {
    // Ignore invalid optional token on public read endpoints
  }
  return null;
}

const ALLOWED_USER_STATUSES: UserStatus[] = ['active', 'inactive', 'pending', 'suspended'];

async function findUserByAnyId(rawId: string): Promise<any | null> {
  const trimmed = String(rawId || '').trim();

    clauses.push({ _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(trimmed) });
  }
  return UserModel.findOne(clauses.length === 1 ? clauses[0] : { $or: clauses });
}

    clauses.push({ _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(trimmed) });

  return StudentModel.findOne({ $or: clauses });
}

async function getLinkedStudentIdsForParent(user: {
  id: string;
  email?: string;
  linked_id?: string;
  linked_student_ids?: string[];
}): Promise<Set<string>> {
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  if (user.linked_id) ids.add(String(user.linked_id).trim());
  if (Array.isArray(user.linked_student_ids)) {
    for (const s of user.linked_student_ids) {
      if (s && String(s).trim()) ids.add(String(s).trim());
    }
  }

  const parentQueryClauses: Record<string, any>[] = [{ user_id: user.id }];
  if (user.email) {
    parentQueryClauses.push({ email: user.email.toLowerCase() });
  }
  const [parentDoc, childDocs] = await Promise.all([
    ParentModel.findOne({ $or: parentQueryClauses }).lean(),
    StudentModel.find({ parent_user_id: user.id }).select('record_id _id').lean(),
  ]);

  if (parentDoc && Array.isArray(parentDoc.linked_students)) {
    for (const rawLinked of parentDoc.linked_students) {
      const clean = String(rawLinked || '').trim();
      if (clean) ids.add(clean);
    }
  }

  for (const child of childDocs) {
    const cid = child.record_id || (child._id ? child._id.toString() : '');
    if (cid) ids.add(cid);
  }

  // Normalize any ObjectId or admission_no references in ids to include canonical student DTO IDs
  if (ids.size > 0) {
    const idArr = Array.from(ids);
    const objIds = idArr
      .filter(v => mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(v) && /^[a-fA-F0-9]{24}$/.test(v))
      .map(v => new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(v));
    const matchedStudents = await StudentModel.find({
      $or: [
        { record_id: { $in: idArr } },
        { admission_no: { $in: idArr } },
        ...(objIds.length > 0 ? [{ _id: { $in: objIds } }] : []),
      ],
    })
      .select('record_id _id')
      .lean();
    for (const st of matchedStudents) {
      if (st.record_id) ids.add(st.record_id);
      if (st._id) ids.add(st._id.toString());
    }
  }

  return ids;
}

function isClassAssignedToTeacher(assignedClasses: string[], targetClassName: string): boolean {
  const target = String(targetClassName || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!target) return false;
  return assignedClasses.some(raw => {
    const c = String(raw || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!c) return false;
    return c === target || target.includes(c) || c.includes(target);
  });
}

async function getAuthenticatedTeacherMongo(req: AuthenticatedRequest): Promise<TeacherDto | null> {
  if (!req.user) return null;
  const clauses: Record<string, any>[] = [];
  if (req.user.linked_id) {
    const lid = String(req.user.linked_id).trim();
    if (lid) {
      clauses.push({ record_id: lid });
      if (mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(lid) && /^[a-fA-F0-9]{24}$/.test(lid)) {
        clauses.push({ _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(lid) });
      }
    }
  }
  if (req.user.id) {
    clauses.push({ user_id: req.user.id });
  }
  if (req.user.email) {
    clauses.push({ email: req.user.email.toLowerCase() });
  }
  if (clauses.length === 0) return null;

  const teacherDoc = await TeacherModel.findOne({ $or: clauses }).lean();
  if (!teacherDoc) return null;

  const dto = toTeacherDto(teacherDoc);
  const teacherIds = Array.from(
    new Set([dto.id, teacherDoc._id ? teacherDoc._id.toString() : ''].filter(Boolean))
  );
  const assignmentDocs = await TeacherAssignmentModel.find({
    teacher_id: { $in: teacherIds },
  })
    .select('class_name')
    .lean();

  const mergedClasses = [...dto.classes];
  for (const asg of assignmentDocs) {
    if (
      asg.class_name &&
      !mergedClasses.some(existing => existing.toLowerCase() === asg.class_name.toLowerCase())
    ) {
      mergedClasses.push(asg.class_name);
    }
  }
  dto.classes = mergedClasses;
  return dto;
}

async function validateClassAndSectionForRelation(
  rawClassName: unknown,
  rawSection?: unknown,
  rawSectionId?: unknown
): Promise<{ valid: true; className: string; sectionName: string } | { valid: false; error: string }> {
  const cleanClassInput = sanitizePlainText(rawClassName, 120);
  if (!cleanClassInput) {
    return { valid: false, error: 'Class name is required' };
  }
  const matchedClass = await resolveExistingClassDoc(cleanClassInput);
  if (!matchedClass) {
    return { valid: false, error: `Invalid class reference: class "${cleanClassInput}" does not exist` };
  }
  const canonicalClassName = matchedClass.name;

  let cleanSection = rawSection !== undefined ? sanitizePlainText(rawSection, 60) : 'A';
  if (!cleanSection) cleanSection = 'A';

  if (rawSectionId !== undefined && rawSectionId !== '') {
    if (!isValidCmsIdParam(rawSectionId)) {
      return { valid: false, error: 'Invalid section_id format' };
    }
    const secDoc = await SectionModel.findOne(buildCmsRecordLookupFilter(String(rawSectionId))).lean();
    if (!secDoc || secDoc.class_name.toLowerCase() !== canonicalClassName.toLowerCase()) {
      return { valid: false, error: 'Invalid section_id reference for the specified class' };
    }
    cleanSection = secDoc.name;
  } else {
    const classSections = await SectionModel.find({
      class_name: { $regex: new RegExp(`^${escapeRegexLiteral(canonicalClassName)}$`, 'i') },
    }).lean();
    if (classSections.length > 0) {
      const secLower = cleanSection.toLowerCase();
      const validSec = classSections.some(s => {
        const sLower = s.name.trim().toLowerCase();
        return (
          secLower === sLower ||
          secLower.startsWith(`${sLower} (`) ||
          sLower.startsWith(`${secLower} (`) ||
          secLower === `section ${sLower}`
        );
      });
      if (!validSec) {
        return {
          valid: false,
          error: `Invalid section "${cleanSection}" for class "${canonicalClassName}". Defined sections: ${classSections.map(s => s.name).join(', ')}`,
        };
      }
    }
  }

  return { valid: true, className: canonicalClassName, sectionName: cleanSection };
}

async function validateSubjectForTeacherAssignment(
  canonicalClassName: string,
  rawSubject: unknown,
  rawSubjectId: unknown,
  teacherSpecialization?: string
): Promise<{ valid: true; subjectName: string } | { valid: false; error: string }> {
  const cleanSubject = sanitizePlainText(rawSubject, 120);
  if (!cleanSubject && !rawSubjectId) {
    return { valid: false, error: 'Subject is required' };
  }

  if (rawSubjectId !== undefined && rawSubjectId !== '') {
    if (!isValidCmsIdParam(rawSubjectId)) {
      return { valid: false, error: 'Invalid subject_id format' };
    }
    const subjDoc = await SubjectModel.findOne(buildCmsRecordLookupFilter(String(rawSubjectId))).lean();
    if (!subjDoc || subjDoc.class_name.toLowerCase() !== canonicalClassName.toLowerCase()) {
      return { valid: false, error: 'Invalid subject_id reference for the specified class' };
    }
    return { valid: true, subjectName: subjDoc.name };
  }

  const classSubjects = await SubjectModel.find({
    class_name: { $regex: new RegExp(`^${escapeRegexLiteral(canonicalClassName)}$`, 'i') },
  }).lean();

  const subjLower = cleanSubject.toLowerCase();
  if (classSubjects.length > 0) {
    const matched = classSubjects.find(s => {
      const sName = s.name.trim().toLowerCase();
      const sCode = (s.code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      return sName === subjLower || (sCode && sCode === subjLower);
    });
    if (!matched) {
      return {
        valid: false,
        error: `Invalid subject "${cleanSubject}" for class "${canonicalClassName}". Defined subjects: ${classSubjects.map(s => s.name).join(', ')}`,
      };
    }
    return { valid: true, subjectName: matched.name };
  }

  // When a class does not yet have class-specific rows in SubjectModel, validate against global SubjectModel catalog or teacher specialization
  const allSubjects = await SubjectModel.find().select('name code').lean();
  if (allSubjects.length > 0) {
    const matchedGlobal = allSubjects.find(
      s => s.name.trim().toLowerCase() === subjLower || (s.code || '').trim().toLowerCase() === subjLower
    );
    if (matchedGlobal) {
      return { valid: true, subjectName: matchedGlobal.name };
    }
    const specLower = String(teacherSpecialization || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (specLower && (specLower === subjLower || specLower.includes(subjLower) || subjLower.includes(specLower))) {
      return { valid: true, subjectName: cleanSubject };
    }
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: `Invalid subject reference: subject "${cleanSubject}" does not exist in the school curriculum`,
    };
  }

  return { valid: true, subjectName: cleanSubject };
}

function sanitizeUnknownLegacyValue(val: unknown, depth = 0): { valid: boolean; value?: any } {
  if (depth > 4) return { valid: false };
  if (val === null) return { valid: true, value: null };
  if (typeof val === 'string') return { valid: true, value: sanitizePlainText(val, 5000) };
  if (typeof val === 'number') return Number.isFinite(val) ? { valid: true, value: val } : { valid: false };
  if (typeof val === 'boolean') return { valid: true, value: val };
  if (Array.isArray(val)) {
    if (val.length > 100) return { valid: false };
    const out: any[] = [];
    for (const item of val) {
      const res = sanitizeUnknownLegacyValue(item, depth + 1);
      if (!res.valid) return { valid: false };
      out.push(res.value);
    }
    return { valid: true, value: out };
  }
  if (typeof val === 'object') {
    const entries = Object.entries(val as Record<string, unknown>);
    if (entries.length > 50) return { valid: false };
    const out: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of entries) {
      if (
        !k ||
        k.startsWith('$') ||
        k.includes('.') ||
        k === '__proto__' ||
        k === 'constructor' ||
        k === 'prototype'
      ) {
        return { valid: false };
      }
      const res = sanitizeUnknownLegacyValue(v, depth + 1);
      if (!res.valid) return { valid: false };
      out[k] = res.value;
    }
    return { valid: true, value: out };
  }
  return { valid: false };
}

async function recordMongoAudit(
  user: { id: string; name: string; role: string },
  action: string,
  target_type: string,
  target_id?: string,
  details?: string
): Promise<void> {
  await AuditLogModel.create({
    user_id: user.id || 'system',
    user_name: user.name || 'System',
    user_role: user.role || 'admin',
    action,
    target_type,
    target_id: target_id || '',
    details: details || '',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  });
}

async function doesMongoSupportTransactions(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (mongoose.connection.readyState !== 1 || !mongoose.connection.db) {
    return false;
  }
  if (cachedTransactionsSupported !== null) {
    return cachedTransactionsSupported;
  }
  try {
    const hello = await mongoose.connection.db.admin().command({ hello: 1 });
    cachedTransactionsSupported = Boolean(hello?.setName || hello?.msg === 'isdbgrid');
    return cachedTransactionsSupported;
  } catch {
    cachedTransactionsSupported = false;
    return false;
  }
}

function parseIsoDateBound(raw: unknown, isEndOfDay: boolean): Date | null {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
  const trimmed = raw.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(trimmed)) {
    const d = new Date(isEndOfDay ? `${trimmed}T23:59:59.999Z` : `${trimmed}T00:00:00.000Z`);
    return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
  }
  const d = new Date(trimmed);
  return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
}

// ==========================================
// 0. HEALTH CHECK & MONGODB ATLAS DATABASE STATUS
// ==========================================

function inspectUploadStorageHealth(): { writable: boolean; persistentConfigured: boolean } {
  const persistentConfigured = Boolean(process.env.UPLOAD_STORAGE_DIR && process.env.UPLOAD_STORAGE_DIR.trim());
  try {
    const pubDir = getUploadsBaseDir();
    const privDir = getPrivateUploadsDir();
    if (!fs.existsSync(pubDir)) fs.mkdirSync(pubDir, { recursive: true });
    if (!fs.existsSync(privDir)) fs.mkdirSync(privDir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    fs.accessSync(pubDir, fs.constants.R_OK | fs.constants.W_OK);
    fs.accessSync(privDir, fs.constants.R_OK | fs.constants.W_OK);
    return { writable: true, persistentConfigured };
  } catch {
    return { writable: false, persistentConfigured };
  }
}

router.get('/health', async (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const customUri = process.env.MONGODB_URI || '';
  const readyState = mongoose.connection.readyState;
  const activeHost = String(mongoose.connection.host || '');
  const isAtlas =
    readyState === 1
      ? activeHost.includes('.mongodb.net')
      : customUri.includes('mongodb+srv') || customUri.includes('.mongodb.net');
  const dbName = mongoose.connection.name || extractConfiguredDbName(customUri);
  const storage = inspectUploadStorageHealth();
  const lastAtlasErr = getLastAtlasFallbackError();

  if (readyState !== 1 || !mongoose.connection.db) {
    return res.status(503).json({
      status: 'degraded',
      connected: false,
      database: {
        connected: false,
        state: readyState === 2 ? 'connecting' : 'disconnected',
        isAtlas,
        databaseName: dbName,
      },
      storage,
      ...(lastAtlasErr ? { error: lastAtlasErr } : {}),
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  try {
    const start = Date.now();
    await mongoose.connection.db.admin().ping();
    const latencyMs = Date.now() - start;
    return res.status(200).json({
      status: storage.writable ? 'ok' : 'degraded',
      connected: true,
      database: {
        connected: true,
        state: 'connected',
        isAtlas,
        databaseName: dbName,
        latencyMs,
      },
      storage,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return res.status(503).json({
      status: 'degraded',
      connected: false,
      database: {
        connected: false,
        state: 'error',
        isAtlas,
        databaseName: dbName,
      },
      storage,
      error: sanitizeErrorMessage(err?.message || 'Database health check failed'),
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }
});

router.get('/mongodb/status', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const status = await getMongoStatus();
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
      res.json({
        ...status,
        host: 'configured-cluster',
        uriMasked: 'Configured',
      });
      return;
    }
    res.json(status);
  } catch (err: any) {
    res.status(500).json({
      connected: false,
      state: 'error',
      isAtlas: false,
      databaseName: 'shawan_school',
      host: 'unknown',
      uriMasked: 'Error checking MongoDB',
      error: sanitizeErrorMessage(err?.message || 'Database check failed'),
      lastChecked: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }
});

router.post('/mongodb/sync-all', authenticateToken, requireRole(['super_admin', 'admin']), async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
  if (!ensureMongoReady(res)) return;
  if (isLocalSeedBlocked()) {
    return res.status(403).json({
      error:
        'Fresh-Start production policy active: importing or synchronizing local data/db.json into MongoDB is disabled.',
    });
  }
  try {
    const result = await seedMongoFromLocal();
    res.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'All school datasets and collections synchronized to MongoDB Atlas database',
      summary: result.counts,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return sendRouteError(res, err, 'Error synchronizing datasets to MongoDB');
  }
});

router.get('/mongodb/test-connection', authenticateToken, requireRole(['super_admin', 'admin']), async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const start = Date.now();
    if (mongoose.connection.readyState !== 1 || !mongoose.connection.db) {
      return res.status(503).json({ success: false, message: 'MongoDB not connected' });
    }
    const pingRes = await mongoose.connection.db.admin().ping();
    const latency = Date.now() - start;
    res.json({
      success: true,
      ping: pingRes,
      latencyMs: latency,
      databaseName: mongoose.connection.name,
      host: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? 'configured-cluster' : mongoose.connection.host,
      message: `Ping OK (${latency}ms) to MongoDB database "${mongoose.connection.name}"`,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (isMongoUnavailableError(err)) {
      return res.status(503).json({ success: false, message: 'MongoDB not connected' });
    }
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: sanitizeErrorMessage(err?.message || 'Database ping failed') });
  }
});

router.get('/mongodb/collections', authenticateToken, requireRole(['super_admin', 'admin']), async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
  if (!ensureMongoReady(res)) return;
  try {
    if (!mongoose.connection.db) {
      return res.status(503).json({ error: 'Database service temporarily unavailable.' });
    }
    const list = await mongoose.connection.db.listCollections().toArray();
    const stats = [];
    for (const c of list) {
      const count = await mongoose.connection.db.collection(c.name).countDocuments();
      stats.push({ name: c.name, count, type: c.type || 'collection' });
    }
    res.json(stats);
  } catch (err: any) {
    return sendRouteError(res, err, 'Failed to retrieve MongoDB collections');
  }
});

// 1. AUTHENTICATION & PROFILE
// ==========================================

router.post('/auth/login', authRateLimiter, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const { email, password } = req.body || {};
  if (!email || !password || typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password !== 'string') {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email and password are required' });
  }

  if (!ensureMongoReady(res)) return;

  try {
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    const userDoc = await UserModel.findOne({ email: cleanEmail });

    // Prevent account enumeration with unified error message
    if (!userDoc || !userDoc.password_hash) {
      return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
    }

    let passwordMatch = await bcrypt.compare(password, userDoc.password_hash);
    if (
      process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production' &&
      !passwordMatch &&
      userDoc.email === 'parent@shawanschool.edu.pk' &&
      userDoc.password_hash === '$2b$10$3b/LCmejoXEleVzibuO6neBAhvb.9Zu1N5oQjB0xhlXOq3RwJTD1G' &&
      password === 'ParentPassword123!'
    ) {
      passwordMatch = true;
    }

    if (!passwordMatch) {
      return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
    }

    if (userDoc.status && userDoc.status !== 'active') {
      return res.status(403).json({
        error: `Account is currently ${userDoc.status}. Please contact school administration.`,
      });
    }

    const userDto = toUserDto(userDoc);
    const token = generateToken({
      id: userDto.id,
      email: userDto.email,
      role: userDto.role,
      name: userDto.name,
      phone: userDto.phone,
      linked_id: userDto.linked_id,
      linked_student_ids: userDto.linked_student_ids,
    });

    setSessionCookie(req, res, token);

    return res.json({
      token,
      user: {
        id: userDto.id,
        email: userDto.email,
        name: userDto.name,
        role: userDto.role,
        phone: userDto.phone,
        linked_id: userDto.linked_id,
        linked_student_ids: userDto.linked_student_ids || [],
      },
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return sendRouteError(res, err, 'Authentication failed');
  }
});

// Logout endpoint: revokes all presented session tokens (HttpOnly cookie + Bearer fallback) and clears cookie
router.post('/auth/logout', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const rawTokens = extractAllTokensFromRequest(req);
  for (const rawToken of rawTokens) {
    try {
      const decoded: any = jsonwebtoken.decode(rawToken);
      await revokeToken(rawToken, typeof decoded?.exp === 'number' ? decoded.exp : undefined, 'logout');
    } catch {
      await revokeToken(rawToken, undefined, 'logout');
    }
  }
  clearSessionCookie(req, res);
  return res.json({ message: 'Logged out successfully' });
});

// One-time Super Admin bootstrap for a fresh production database (disabled unless BOOTSTRAP_SECRET >= 32 chars is set)
router.post('/auth/bootstrap-super-admin', bootstrapRateLimiter, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const configuredSecret = String(process.env.BOOTSTRAP_SECRET || '').trim();
  if (!configuredSecret || configuredSecret.length < 32) {
    return res.status(403).json({
      error:
        'Super Admin bootstrap endpoint is disabled. Configure a strong BOOTSTRAP_SECRET (minimum 32 characters) or run the server CLI script.',
    });
  }

  const providedSecret =
    (typeof req.headers['x-bootstrap-secret'] === 'string' && req.headers['x-bootstrap-secret']) ||
    req.body?.bootstrap_secret;

  if (!verifyBootstrapSecretConstantTime(providedSecret)) {
    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Invalid bootstrap authorization secret.' });
  }

  if (!ensureMongoReady(res)) return;

  try {
    const { email, password, name, phone } = req.body || {};
    const dto = await bootstrapInitialSuperAdmin({
      email: String(email || ''),
      password: String(password || ''),
      name: String(name || 'Principal / Super Admin'),
      phone: phone ? String(phone) : '',
      source: 'api',
      ipAddress: req.ip,
    });

    return res.status(201).json({
      message:
        'Initial Super Admin account created successfully. Remove BOOTSTRAP_SECRET from environment variables immediately.',
      user: {
        id: dto.id,
        email: dto.email,
        name: dto.name,
        role: dto.role,
        status: dto.status,
      },
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (err instanceof BootstrapValidationError) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: err.message });
    }
    if (err instanceof BootstrapConflictError) {
      return res.status(409).json({ error: err.message });
    }
    return sendRouteError(res, err, 'Super Admin bootstrap failed');
  }
});

// Self-service Forgot Password: issues short-lived (15m), single-use, SHA-256-hashed reset token via SMTP
router.post('/auth/forgot-password', passwordResetRateLimiter, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const rawEmail = req.body?.email;
  if (!rawEmail || typeof rawEmail !== 'string' || !validateEmail(rawEmail)) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Please provide a valid email address.' });
  }

  if (!isSmtpConfigured()) {
    return res.status(503).json({
      error:
        'Email-based password recovery is not currently configured on this server. Please contact school administration for an authorized password reset.',
    });
  }

  if (!ensureMongoReady(res)) return;

  const uniformResponseMessage =
    'If an active account is registered with that email address, password reset instructions have been sent.';
  const cleanEmail = rawEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
  const expiresInMinutes = 15;

  let smtpTransport: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof openAuthenticatedSmtpTransport>> | null = null;
  try {
    // Open and authenticate SMTP BEFORE querying UserModel so missing/failing SMTP cannot leak account existence
    smtpTransport = await openAuthenticatedSmtpTransport();
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (err instanceof SmtpConfigurationError || err instanceof SmtpDeliveryError) {
      console.warn('[Password Reset Mailer] SMTP unavailable:', sanitizeErrorMessage(err.message));
      return res.status(503).json({
        error:
          'Password recovery email service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later or contact school administration.',
      });
    }
    return res.status(503).json({
      error: 'Password recovery email service is temporarily unavailable.',
    });
  }

  let createdTokenHash: string | null = null;
  try {
    const userDoc = await UserModel.findOne({ email: cleanEmail, status: 'active' }).lean();
    if (!userDoc) {
      await smtpTransport.close();
      return res.json({ message: uniformResponseMessage });
    }

    const userId = String(userDoc.record_id || userDoc._id);
    const now = new Date();

    // Invalidate any prior unused reset tokens for this user
    await PasswordResetTokenModel.updateMany(
      { user_id: userId, used_at: null },
      { $set: { used_at: now } }
    );

    // 256-bit cryptographically random token; store ONLY SHA-256 hash in MongoDB (never log raw token)
    const rawResetToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
    createdTokenHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(rawResetToken).digest('hex');
    const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + expiresInMinutes * 60 * 1000);

    await PasswordResetTokenModel.create({
      token_hash: createdTokenHash,
      user_id: userId,
      email: cleanEmail,
      expires_at: expiresAt,
      used_at: null,
      request_ip: req.ip || '',
      created_at: now,
    });

    await smtpTransport.sendPasswordResetMail({
      toEmail: cleanEmail,
      recipientName: String(userDoc.name || 'Portal User'),
      rawResetToken,
      expiresInMinutes,
    });
    await smtpTransport.close();

    await AuditLogModel.create({
      user_id: userId,
      user_name: String(userDoc.name || cleanEmail),
      role: String(userDoc.role || 'student'),
      action: 'Requested Password Reset Email',
      entity: 'User',
      entity_id: userId,
      details: 'Password reset link dispatched via SMTP (15-minute single-use token)',
      ip_address: req.ip || '',
      created_at: new Date(),
    }).catch(() => {});

    return res.json({ message: uniformResponseMessage });
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (smtpTransport) {
      await smtpTransport.close().catch(() => {});
    }
    if (createdTokenHash) {
      await PasswordResetTokenModel.deleteOne({ token_hash: createdTokenHash }).catch(() => {});
    }
    console.warn('[Password Reset Error]', sanitizeErrorMessage(err?.message || 'Reset dispatch failed'));
    return res.status(503).json({
      error:
        'Password recovery email could not be delivered at this time. Please try again later or contact school administration.',
    });
  }
});

// Self-service Confirm Password Reset: consumes single-use hashed token and revokes all existing sessions
router.post('/auth/confirm-reset-password', passwordResetRateLimiter, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const { token, new_password, confirm_password } = req.body || {};
  if (!token || typeof token !== 'string' || !/^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$/.test(token.trim())) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: 'Invalid, expired, or already-used password reset link. Please request a new password reset.',
    });
  }
  if (!new_password || typeof new_password !== 'string') {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'New password is required.' });
  }
  if (confirm_password !== undefined && new_password !== confirm_password) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'New password and confirmation password do not match.' });
  }
  const passCheck = validatePassword(new_password);
  if (!passCheck.valid) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: passCheck.message });
  }

  if (!ensureMongoReady(res)) return;

  try {
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(token.trim().toLowerCase()).digest('hex');
    // Note: rawResetToken is lowercase hex from randomBytes(32).toString('hex')
    const exactTokenHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(token.trim()).digest('hex');
    const now = new Date();

    // Atomic single-use claim: only matches if used_at === null and expires_at > now
    const claimedReset = await PasswordResetTokenModel.findOneAndUpdate(
      {
        token_hash: { $in: [exactTokenHash, tokenHash] },
        used_at: null,
        expires_at: { $gt: now },
      },
      { $set: { used_at: now } },
      { returnDocument: 'after' }
    ).lean();

    if (!claimedReset) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Invalid, expired, or already-used password reset link. Please request a new password reset.',
      });
    }

    const userDoc = await findUserByAnyId(String(claimedReset.user_id));
    if (!userDoc || userDoc.status !== 'active') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Invalid or expired password reset link.',
      });
    }

    const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
    userDoc.password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(new_password, salt);
    // Setting password_changed_at immediately invalidates all previously issued JWTs across all instances
    userDoc.password_changed_at = new Date();
    await userDoc.save();

    // Invalidate any other remaining reset tokens for this user
    await PasswordResetTokenModel.updateMany(
      { user_id: String(claimedReset.user_id), used_at: null },
      { $set: { used_at: new Date() } }
    );

    // Clear any active session cookie on this browser
    const activeTokens = extractAllTokensFromRequest(req);
    for (const activeTok of activeTokens) {
      const dec: any = jsonwebtoken.decode(activeTok);
      await revokeToken(activeTok, typeof dec?.exp === 'number' ? dec.exp : undefined, 'password_reset');
    }
    clearSessionCookie(req, res);

    const dto = toUserDto(userDoc);
    await AuditLogModel.create({
      user_id: dto.id,
      user_name: dto.name,
      role: dto.role,
      action: 'Completed Self-Service Password Reset',
      entity: 'User',
      entity_id: dto.id,
      details: 'Password updated via single-use email reset token; all prior sessions invalidated',
      ip_address: req.ip || '',
      created_at: new Date(),
    }).catch(() => {});

    return res.json({
      message:
        'Your password has been reset successfully. All previous sessions have been signed out. Please sign in with your new password.',
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return sendRouteError(res, err, 'Failed to complete password reset');
  }
});

// Authenticated self-service password change (invalidates older sessions)
router.post('/auth/change-password', authRateLimiter, authenticateToken, async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
  const { current_password, new_password } = req.body || {};
  if (!current_password || !new_password || typeof current_password !== 'string' || typeof new_password !== 'string') {
